\\cos(0) + C_1 = 2\\pi(1) + C_1 = 2\\pi[/math]\u003Cbr\u003E, তাই [math]C_1=0[/math]\u003Cbr\u003E.[math]s(0) = \\sin(0) + C_1(0) + C_2 = 0 + 0 + C_2 = 0[/math]\u003Cbr\u003E, তাই
